<tr>
<th scope="col" style="left:0pt;top:0pt;width:97.5118pt;height:20.95pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="59.6179" style="left:5pt;top:4.5997pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Je berekent…</span></p>
</th>
<th scope="col" style="left:97.5118pt;top:0pt;width:307.1622pt;height:20.95pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="95.6379" style="left:5pt;top:4.5997pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Zo lees je de uitkomst</span></p>
</th>
<th scope="col" style="left:404.674pt;top:0pt;width:82.8851pt;height:20.95pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="56.9155" style="left:5pt;top:4.5997pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Terugzoeken</span></p>
</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="left:0pt;top:20.95pt;width:97.5118pt;height:44.48pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="13.3706" style="left:5pt;top:4.9008pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Ev</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:97.5118pt;top:20.95pt;width:307.1622pt;height:44.48pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.2881" data-layout-width="206.7206" style="left:4.9999pt;top:4.9pt;line-height:11.46pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.55pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">−1 &lt; Ev &lt; 0: prijsinelastisch; Ev = 0: geen reactie.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.2881" data-layout-width="107.3051" style="left:4.9999pt;top:16.55pt;line-height:11.46pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.55pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Ev = −1: unitair elastisch.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.2881" data-layout-width="95.5204" style="left:4.9999pt;top:28.2pt;line-height:11.46pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.55pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Ev &lt; −1: prijselastisch.</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:404.674pt;top:20.95pt;width:82.8851pt;height:44.48pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="28.85" style="left:5pt;top:4.9008pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">§2.2.1</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="42.0758" style="left:5pt;top:16.5518pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">p. 37–38</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="left:0pt;top:65.43pt;width:97.5118pt;height:32.75pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="10.9164" style="left:5pt;top:4.8237pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Ei</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:97.5118pt;top:65.43pt;width:307.1622pt;height:32.75pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="294.6649" style="left:5pt;top:4.8237pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">In dit boek: Ei &lt; 0 inferieur; 0 &lt; Ei &lt; 1 normaal; Ei &gt; 1 luxe. Ei = 0 en Ei</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="96.6025" style="left:5pt;top:16.4747pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">= 1 zijn grenswaarden.</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:404.674pt;top:65.43pt;width:82.8851pt;height:32.75pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="28.85" style="left:5pt;top:4.8237pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">§2.2.3</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="24.1231" style="left:5pt;top:16.4747pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">p. 53</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="left:0pt;top:98.18pt;width:97.5118pt;height:44.41pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="13.5806" style="left:5pt;top:4.8257pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Ek</span></p>
</td>

<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="98.7128" style="left:5pt;top:16.4767pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Ek &lt; 0: complementen.</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="151.3582" style="left:5pt;top:28.1278pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Ek = 0: geen reactie in deze meting.</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:404.674pt;top:98.18pt;width:82.8851pt;height:44.41pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="28.85" style="left:5pt;top:4.8257pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">§2.2.3</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="24.1231" style="left:5pt;top:16.4767pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">p. 54</span></p>

<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.7595" data-layout-width="123.2159" style="left:53.8583pt;top:405.3575pt;line-height:14.7595pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.2996pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Percentages en omzet</span></h2>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.3198" data-layout-width="123.8663" style="left:64.8583pt;top:433.4127pt;line-height:13.3198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.0999pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Procentuele verandering</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="16.3198" data-layout-width="90.478" style="left:311.1378pt;top:433.4127pt;line-height:13.3198pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:11.0999pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Omzet vóór en na</span></p>
<span aria-label="nieuw − oud gedeeld door oud" class="native-fraction" role="math" style="left:66.8583pt;top:451.5138pt;width:78.2021pt;height:36.0196pt"><span class="native-text numerator" data-layout-height="18.8396" data-layout-width="75.2288" style="left:3pt;top:0pt;line-height:15.8396pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:13.1997pt;font-weight:700;color:#007da2">nieuw − oud</span></span><span class="native-text denominator" data-layout-height="18.8396" data-layout-width="25.5319" style="left:27.8419pt;top:20.1799pt;line-height:15.8396pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:13.1997pt;font-weight:700;color:#007da2">oud</span></span><span aria-hidden="true" class="fraction-rule" style="top:17.5098pt;border-top:1pt solid #007da2"></span></span>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="18.8396" data-layout-width="67.8765" style="left:311.1378pt;top:452.4378pt;line-height:15.8396pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:13.1997pt;font-weight:700;color:#008779">TO = P × Q</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="18.8396" data-layout-width="50.849" style="left:147.0604pt;top:459.8813pt;line-height:15.8396pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:13.1997pt;font-weight:700;color:#007da2"> × 100%</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="18.8396" data-layout-width="52.1425" style="left:311.1378pt;top:474.4658pt;line-height:15.8396pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:13.1997pt;font-weight:700;color:#008779">%ΔTO =</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="41.5556" data-layout-width="217.7275" style="left:64.8583pt;top:492.6629pt;line-height:13.158pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.1997pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Gebruik de </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.1997pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">oude waarde</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.1997pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51"> van de grootheid die je</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.1997pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">onderzoekt. Elasticiteiten zelf hebben geen</span><br/><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.1997pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">eenheid.</span></p>
<span aria-label="TO nieuw − TO oud gedeeld door TO oud" class="native-fraction" role="math" style="left:313.1378pt;top:495.5698pt;width:120.75pt;height:36.0196pt"><span class="native-text numerator" data-layout-height="18.8396" data-layout-width="117.7847" style="left:3pt;top:0pt;line-height:15.8396pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:13.1997pt;font-weight:700;color:#008779">TO nieuw − TO oud</span></span><span class="native-text denominator" data-layout-height="18.8396" data-layout-width="47.0078" style="left:38.3782pt;top:20.18pt;line-height:15.8396pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:13.1997pt;font-weight:700;color:#008779">TO oud</span></span><span aria-hidden="true" class="fraction-rule" style="top:17.5098pt;border-top:1.0001pt solid #008779"></span></span>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="18.8396" data-layout-width="50.8489" style="left:435.8878pt;top:503.9373pt;line-height:15.8396pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:13.1997pt;font-weight:700;color:#008779"> × 100%</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="15.2397" data-layout-width="214.3275" style="left:311.1378pt;top:536.7189pt;line-height:12.2396pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.1997pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Prijs en hoeveelheid horen bij </span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.1997pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">dezelfde situatie</span><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:10.1997pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">.</span></p>
<h2 class="native-text" data-layout-height="17.7595" data-layout-width="231.2553" style="left:53.8583pt;top:564.6025pt;line-height:14.7595pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:12.2996pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Vier controles voor een volledig antwoord</span></h2>

<tr>
<th scope="col" style="left:0pt;top:0pt;width:112.1386pt;height:20.95pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="39.9749" style="left:5pt;top:4.5927pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Controle</span></p>

<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="53.1243" style="left:5pt;top:4.5927pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Wat doe je?</span></p>
</th>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="left:0pt;top:20.95pt;width:112.1386pt;height:32.82pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="45.1698" style="left:5pt;top:4.8938pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Eenheden</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:112.1386pt;top:20.95pt;width:375.4205pt;height:32.82pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="363.4961" style="left:5pt;top:4.8938pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Maandomzet hoort bij verkochte aantallen per maand. In een functie blijft jaarinkomen</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="74.2188" style="left:5pt;top:16.5448pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">een jaarinkomen.</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="left:0pt;top:53.77pt;width:112.1386pt;height:32.76pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="28.0287" style="left:5pt;top:4.8257pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Teken</span></p>

<td style="left:112.1386pt;top:53.77pt;width:375.4205pt;height:32.76pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.2881" data-layout-width="354.946" style="left:5.0031pt;top:4.83pt;line-height:11.46pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.55pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Houd het teken in Ev, Ei en Ek. Vergelijk Ev met −1 en 0; bij Ei en Ek heeft het teken</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.2881" data-layout-width="152.4862" style="left:5.0031pt;top:16.48pt;line-height:11.46pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.55pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">een andere economische betekenis.</span></p>
</td>
</tr>
<tr>
<td style="left:0pt;top:86.53pt;width:112.1386pt;height:32.75pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="46.5258" style="left:5pt;top:4.8177pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Eén factor</span></p>
</td>
<td style="left:112.1386pt;top:86.53pt;width:375.4205pt;height:32.75pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="363.1236" style="left:5pt;top:4.8177pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">Bereken de beginsituatie. Verander alleen de gevraagde factor. Zet eerdere wijzigingen</span></p>
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="27.4558" style="left:5pt;top:16.4687pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">terug.</span></p>

</tr>
<tr>
<td style="left:0pt;top:119.28pt;width:112.1386pt;height:32.75pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="44.1862" style="left:5pt;top:4.8198pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:700;color:#183e51">Conclusie</span></p>

<td style="left:112.1386pt;top:119.28pt;width:375.4205pt;height:32.75pt">
<p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="354.6058" style="left:5pt;top:4.8198pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">De omzetregel is lokaal. <p class="native-text" data-layout-height="14.4592" data-layout-width="238.6676" style="left:5pt;top:16.4708pt;line-height:11.4592pt"><span style="font-family:Lato;font-size:9.5493pt;font-weight:400;color:#183e51">geen hogere winst. Een volgende meting kan anders zijn.</span></p>
